=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-mcp@0.2.38 | 2026-11-11 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-mcp%400.2.38

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-langchain@4.2.0 | 2026-11-11 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-langchain%404.2.0
### Minor Changes

- 10bb8cc: Read the LangSmith params (`ls_provider` and `ls_model_name`) that LangChain chat models report in their run metadata, matching the Python instrumentation.
  
  - `llm.model_name` now comes from the model the response reports in `llmOutput`, then `ls_model_name`, then the invocation params' `model_name` or `model`, which is the Python instrumentation's order. Chat models that report their model only as `ls_model_name`, e.g. `@langchain/google`'s, now get a model name, so cost tracking can price their spans. Blank names are skipped, and the model name no longer depends on the run having invocation params.
  - `llm.provider` and `llm.system` are now set from `ls_provider`, using the Python instrumentation's mappings (e.g. `google_genai` → `google` / `vertexai`, `azure` → `azure` / `openai`) plus the JS-only `cerebras`, `mistral` and `watsonx`. Other providers are recorded as their lowercased `ls_provider`.

### Patch Changes

- Updated dependencies [10bb8cc]
  - @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.16.0
  - @arizeai/openinference-core@2.8.2

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-anthropic@1.0.1 | 2026-11-05 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-anthropic%401.0.1
### Patch Changes

- 7abd8ec: Stop `@anthropic-ai/sdk` >= 0.134.0 from adding a second span to each traced Messages call. The SDK now starts a span of its own for every API call, so `messages.create()`, `messages.stream()` and `countTokens()` (stable and beta) each exported an OpenInference LLM span and an SDK `anthropic.messages.*` span, and the `messages.stream()` span was not under the LLM span. A traced call now hands the SDK a non-recording span with the LLM span's context, so only the LLM span is recorded, the request still carries the trace context, and HTTP instrumentation spans nest under the LLM span. SDK calls that are not traced keep their spans.

* **openai-go:** record Responses streams that end incomplete or failed ([#4105](https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/issues/4105)) ([f6c4763](https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/commit/f6c476333bee3a4124c279e9c4dfaf45294dcd2b))

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-openai@4.4.3 | 2026-11-04 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-openai%404.4.3
### Patch Changes

- 07ce304: Record the output and token counts of OpenAI Responses streams that end with `response.incomplete` or `response.failed`. Previously only `response.completed` was kept, so the span ended with an error status and no output or usage. A failed response still sets an error status; an incomplete one (for example `max_output_tokens`) is recorded like a completed one.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-vercel@3.3.1 | 2026-10-23 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-vercel%403.3.1
### Patch Changes

- ca8a7e9: Keep classifying Vercel eve's `agent.step` and `agent.approval` spans as CHAIN on eve 0.76 and later. eve 0.76 sets `operation.name` and `gen_ai.operation.name` on these spans to `workflow` and moves the span name to `resource.name`, so they no longer matched the eve span-kind map and fell through to the GenAI converter's LLM default, which doubled a turn's LLM span count. When `gen_ai.operation.name` is `workflow`, the processor now also matches `resource.name` against that map. Every other span classifies as before.
- 5a02f5b: Declare `@opentelemetry/sdk-trace-base` as a dependency. The span processors import it at runtime, but it was only a devDependency, so apps had to install it themselves. OpenTelemetry JS SDK 3.0 removed `sdk-trace-base`, and the remaining 2.x releases peer `@opentelemetry/api` below 1.10, so with the latest packages the import failed (`ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND`) or the install failed (ERESOLVE). The package now installs its own 2.x copy.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-anthropic@1.0.0 | 2026-10-23 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-anthropic%401.0.0
### Major Changes

- 38b858d: Support the current Anthropic Messages API (stable and beta, `@anthropic-ai/sdk` 0.133)
  
  Behavior changes to note:
  
  - A streamed response's `output.value` is now the message rebuilt from the stream as JSON (`output.mime_type` `application/json`, previously the concatenated text as `text/plain`), the same as for a non-streaming response.
  - The top-level `system` prompt is now recorded as input message 0, so the conversation's messages start at index 1 when a system prompt is set. It is no longer part of `llm.invocation_parameters`, which `hideInputs` does not hide.
  
  Changes:
  
  - Streams are observed through the caller's own iterator instead of a tee, so a mid-stream error no longer causes an unhandled rejection, `break` still aborts the request, nothing is buffered, and the span ends OK, unset (left early or aborted) or ERROR. Streamed tool input is parsed once per block.
  - Attribute extraction can no longer throw into the caller.
  - Server and MCP tool calls are recorded as tool calls; compaction blocks as text; every other content block (tool results, documents, MCP listings, container uploads, unknown types) as its type and JSON, with embedded base64 data replaced by a placeholder and nested images hidden by `hideInputImages`.
  - Citations, MCP server names, tool call callers and toolset names, `stop_details`, cache miss reasons, the container id, context management edits, input transformations, iteration usage and server tool usage go to span metadata, merged with the context metadata. Citations are left out when inputs, outputs, input messages or output messages are hidden, and a citation's `cited_text` when input text or output text are hidden, and a refusal's `explanation` when outputs, output messages or output text are hidden.
  - Images record URLs and base64 data URIs (Files API images are recorded as their JSON); `llm.tools` records `tool.name` and `tool.description`, and applies the request's `tool_addition` and `tool_removal` blocks.
  - Thinking tokens are recorded as `llm.token_count.completion_details.reasoning`.
  - `llm.provider` is `aws`, `google` or `azure` for the Bedrock, Vertex and Foundry clients.
  - `messages.countTokens` / `beta.messages.countTokens` are traced, and each run of `beta.messages.toolRunner` runs under a `beta.messages.tool_runner` CHAIN span (the naming proposed for the Python instrumentation in PR #3531) parenting its LLM spans and a TOOL span per tool run, including tools added with `addTools()` or `setMessagesParams()`.

### Patch Changes

- 2925fc3: Record every `tool_result` block of an input message as its own tool message. Previously only the last block survived on the span, so parallel tool-call replies lost all but one result. Tool messages are recorded ahead of any other content in the same message, matching the order the API requires.

  - @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.15.0
  - @arizeai/openinference-core@2.8.1

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-core@2.8.1 | 2026-10-23 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-core%402.8.1
### Patch Changes

- 298ff79: Read the `OPENINFERENCE_*` environment variables when no `traceConfig` is passed. `generateTraceConfig()` returned the hard-coded defaults for an undefined config, so `OPENINFERENCE_HIDE_INPUTS=true` and the other masking variables had no effect on any instrumentation constructed without a `traceConfig`.
- Updated dependencies [dc63d01]
  - @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.15.0

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-vercel@3.3.0 | 2026-10-09 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-vercel%403.3.0
### Minor Changes

- 2ce4ede: Add OpenInference decision model telemetry for AI SDK v7 `experimental_decide` calls. Decision model calls become `DECISION` spans with `decision.*` model, provider, system, and token count attributes, and the new `enrichSpanWithOpenInference` callback (pass it as `OpenTelemetry`'s `enrichSpan` option; requires `ai` 7.0.128 and `@ai-sdk/otel` 1.0.128 or later) classifies the outer decide operation as a `CHAIN` so that model identity and usage are not duplicated. The GenAI model and usage attributes (`gen_ai.provider.name`, `gen_ai.request.model`, `gen_ai.response.model`, `gen_ai.usage.*`) are removed from decision spans after mapping so that GenAI converters do not re-label the decision model as an LLM.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-openai@4.4.1 | 2026-10-09 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-openai%404.4.1
### Patch Changes

- 744ab39: End the span with an ERROR status and an `exception` event when the request made by `chat.completions.create`, `completions.create`, `embeddings.create` or `responses.create` fails (for example a 404, 429, 5xx, timeout or abort). Previously the span was never ended, so failed calls were missing from traces.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-vercel@3.2.3 | 2026-10-01 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-vercel%403.2.3
### Patch Changes

- 945c024: Classify Vercel eve's `agent.step`, `agent.action`, and `agent.approval` control-flow spans as CHAIN instead of LLM (an `agent.action` for a subagent or remote-agent call stays AGENT). They carry `gen_ai.*` context but no `gen_ai.operation.name`, so they fell through to the GenAI converter's LLM default and inflated the LLM span count well beyond the number of model calls. A `session.id` already on a span (for example from `setSession` context) now takes precedence over the one derived from `gen_ai.conversation.id`.
- Updated dependencies [53b7a0e]
- Updated dependencies [945c024]
  - @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.14.0
  - @arizeai/openinference-genai@0.4.0
  - @arizeai/openinference-core@2.7.3

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-vercel@3.2.2 | 2026-10-01 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-vercel%403.2.2
### Patch Changes

- 300bba9: Read the parent span from `parentSpanContext` (OpenTelemetry JS SDK 2.x) with a fallback to `parentSpanId` (SDK 1.x) when deciding whether a span is the trace root. Under SDK 2.x every span was treated as a root, so child AI SDK spans were renamed to their `operation.name`, a later successful child span inherited an earlier sibling's ERROR status, and nested kind-less AI spans were promoted to AGENT when `reparentOrphanedSpans` is enabled.
- Updated dependencies [a1f276c]
- Updated dependencies [a719562]
  - @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.13.0
  - @arizeai/openinference-core@2.7.2
  - @arizeai/openinference-genai@0.3.11

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.14.0 | 2026-10-01 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions%402.14.0
### Minor Changes

- 53b7a0e: Add `decision.model_name`, `decision.request.model_name`, `decision.response.model_name`, `decision.system`, `decision.provider`, `decision.token_count.input`, and `decision.token_count.output` attributes for `DECISION` spans, plus `DecisionSystem` and `DecisionProvider` well-known value enums that alias the matching `LLMSystem` and `LLMProvider` values.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.13.0 | 2026-10-01 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions%402.13.0
### Minor Changes

- a1f276c: Add the `DECISION` span kind to `OpenInferenceSpanKind` for decision model calls that score or select among candidate options.
- a719562: Add `ORACLE` to the `LLMProvider` enum, giving Oracle Cloud Infrastructure Generative AI (`https://inference.generativeai.<region>.oci.oraclecloud.com`) a well-known `llm.provider` value instead of leaving it to a custom string. Mirrors the same addition in the Python, Java, and Go semantic conventions and in the spec's well-known value table.

### Patch Changes

- 4157dde: Capture prompt caching token counts as `llm.token_count.prompt_details.cache_write` and `llm.token_count.prompt_details.cache_read` for streaming and non-streaming `messages.create` calls. Anthropic's `input_tokens` excludes cached tokens, so `llm.token_count.prompt` and `llm.token_count.total` now include the cache write and read counts. Zero cache counts are omitted, and streaming usage is merged field by field across `message_start`, the server-side fallback hop and `message_delta`, so streaming and non-streaming spans report the same counts.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.9.0 | 2026-09-04 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions%402.9.0
### Minor Changes

- fd01216: Add `META`, `ZAI`, and `MINIMAX` to the `LLMProvider` enum, giving Meta AI (`https://api.meta.ai/v1`, `muse-spark-*` models), Z.ai (`https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4`, GLM models), and MiniMax (`https://api.minimax.io/v1`) well-known `llm.provider` values instead of leaving each to a custom string. Mirrors the same additions in the Python, Java, and Go semantic conventions and in the spec's well-known value table.

=== RELEASE @arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-openai@4.2.4 | 2026-09-04 | https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference/releases/tag/%40arizeai/openinference-instrumentation-openai%404.2.4
### Patch Changes

- fd01216: Detect Meta AI, Z.ai, and MiniMax from the request host, so an OpenAI client pointed at one of their OpenAI-compatible endpoints records the real `llm.provider` instead of falling back to `openai`. Adds `api.meta.ai` → `meta`, `api.z.ai` → `zai`, and `api.minimax.io` / `api.minimaxi.com` / `api.minimax.chat` → `minimax` to `HOST_SUFFIX_TO_PROVIDER`. Matching stays suffix-based and anchored at a label boundary, so subdomains of these hosts resolve too and unrelated hosts are unaffected.
- Updated dependencies [fd01216]
  - @arizeai/openinference-semantic-conventions@2.9.0
  - @arizeai/openinference-core@2.6.2
